- **21-mac**: `{{app_name}}Mac/` — macOS app (`MacRootView`). Shares `{{bundle_root}}` with iOS for Universal Purchase; a split ID breaks IAP entitlement transfer. ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/21-mac.md))
- **21-zero-setup-agent-workspace**: The product contract is that customers should be able to drop agents into a workspace and have those agents set themselves up. The customer should not need to integrate with the product before the product can become useful. ([jscraik/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/jscraik/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/Docs/agents/21-zero-setup-agent-workspace.md) · ★9)
- **21st-component-builder**: Use to generate or install polished React + Tailwind + shadcn/ui components via 21st.dev (Magic MCP or the shadcn registry) and theme them to the Foundry design tokens. &lt;example&gt;Context: fast UI. user: "Build a slick settlements table and a job-intake form." assistant: "I'll use the 21st-component-builder agent to generate them with 21st.dev Magic and theme to our tokens."&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Context: landing blocks. user: "I need a hero and a pricing section." assistant: "Let me use the 21st-component-builder agent to pull 21st.dev blocks and adapt them."&lt;/example&gt; ([Sushant6095/synapse](https://github.com/Sushant6095/synapse/blob/HEAD/agents/21st-component-builder.md))
- **22-systems-thinking-product-rule**: Systems thinking means spotting blockers, designing ways for people and agents to systematically overcome them, and explaining how code makes that possible. ([jscraik/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/jscraik/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/Docs/agents/22-systems-thinking-product-rule.md) · ★9)
- **22-watch**: `{{app_name}}Watch/` — watchOS companion. Each side runs a local store; the WCSession seam pushes phone context, it is not a reconciling sync engine. ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/22-watch.md))
- **23-complications**: `WatchComplications/` — complication timelines. `accessoryCorner` is watchOS-only. ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/23-complications.md))
- **23-ctf-workflow-evals**: High-level workflow skills must close the loop by proving they can complete realistic tasks, not only by sounding plausible. Use capture-the-flag style evals for workflows such as logging in, uploading attachments and starting a chat, or granting a group access to a Workplace Agent. ([jscraik/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/jscraik/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/Docs/agents/23-ctf-workflow-evals.md) · ★9)
- **24-tessl-live-skill-eval-workflow**: Use this workflow when an operator explicitly asks for live Tessl evidence for a skill, or asks to use Tessl's scenario-generation skill to improve the local eval suite. ([jscraik/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/jscraik/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/Docs/agents/24-tessl-live-skill-eval-workflow.md) · ★9)
- **24-widgets-home**: `HomeWidget/` — iOS widgets. Every view calls `.containerBackground(for: .widget)`. ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/24-widgets-home.md))
- **25-sdk-runtime-lane-contract**: The approved separation outcome requires checked private Tessl versions and their installation into the selected Codex home runtimes, preserving verified OpenAI-provider exemptions. See the [approved lifecycle](/Docs/agents/14-path-ownership-boundaries.md#approved-lifecycle). Registry delivery, installed behaviour and repository independence need separate proof; none is optional programme polish. ([jscraik/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/jscraik/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/Docs/agents/25-sdk-runtime-lane-contract.md) · ★9)
- **25-widget-mac**: `MacWidget/` — macOS widget extension, same App Group read path. ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/25-widget-mac.md))
- **26-live-activity**: `LiveActivity/` — Live Activity + Dynamic Island. `SessionActivityAttributes` is guarded `#if os(iOS)`, never `canImport(ActivityKit)` (it imports on macOS). ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/26-live-activity.md))
- **26-pm-thread-coordination**: Use this contract for controller-to-owner coordination. The separation-first contract below replaces the legacy mandatory report workflow for this programme. ([jscraik/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/jscraik/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/Docs/agents/26-pm-thread-coordination.md) · ★9)
- **27-nse**: `NotificationService/` — APNs mutable-content handler; writes `WidgetSync`. `NSSupportsLiveActivities` lives in the HOST plist, never the extension's. ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/27-nse.md))
- **273-disconnected-files**: **For a fresh agent.** You can act on this file with no other context. It is the output of a full audit (30 Aug 2026) plus an adversarial pass that overturned or narrowed 17 of its own 39 verdicts. ([FightTheStroke/MirrorBuddy](https://github.com/FightTheStroke/MirrorBuddy/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/273-disconnected-files.md) · ★8)
- **28-swiftdata**: `Shared/Store/SwiftDataCheckpointStore.swift` — SwiftData persistence. `CheckpointPersisting` is `@MainActor` because `ModelContext` is not `Sendable`. ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/28-swiftdata.md))
- **29-store**: `Shared/Capabilities/Store/` — StoreKit 2 paywall stub (`optional: true` source). ([rex/template-apple-swift](https://github.com/rex/template-apple-swift/blob/HEAD/template/docs/fragments/agents/29-store.md))
- **2_method_interpreter**: Use to label retrieved method papers by component type, modality compatibility, and generality, without proposing methods or research directions. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_method_interpreter.md))
- **2_method_survey_criticism**: Review Method Survey Team outputs and detect ungated searches, unsupported compatibility/generality labels, idea generation, and unnecessary verbosity. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_method_survey_criticism.md))
- **2_method_survey_search**: Use to retrieve papers describing generic methods, architectures, or components compatible with the repository's modality and structure, and summarize the associated technique categories. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_method_survey_search.md))
- **2_method_survey_synthesizer**: Use to merge outputs from the Method Survey Team into a shared method survey, keeping only modality-compatible papers. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_method_survey_synthesizer.md))
- **2_paper_interpreter**: Use to label retrieved papers by alignment with the repository profile, without proposing methods or research directions. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_paper_interpreter.md))
- **2_task_survey_criticism**: Review Task Survey Team outputs and detect unsupported alignment labels, idea generation, and unnecessary verbosity. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_task_survey_criticism.md))
- **2_task_survey_search**: Use to retrieve papers studying the repository task and summarize the associated research directions. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_task_survey_search.md))
- **2_task_survey_synthesizer**: Use to merge outputs from the Task Survey Team into a shared task survey, keeping only aligned papers. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/2_task_survey_synthesizer.md))
- **2b-rust-source-analyzer**: Performs source-level zeroization analysis for Rust crates in zeroize-audit. Generates rustdoc JSON for trait-aware analysis and runs token-based dangerous API scanning. Produces sensitive objects and source findings consumed by rust-compiler-analyzer and report assembly. ([trailofbits/skills](https://github.com/trailofbits/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/zeroize-audit/agents/2b-rust-source-analyzer.md) · ★7.2k · 6 copies)
- **2d-artist**: Owns 2D art pipeline quality — sprites, tilemaps, 2D animations, UI visual assets, and placeholder assets. ([MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code](https://github.com/MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code/blob/HEAD/agents/2d-artist.md) · ★86)
- **2l-builder**: Implements features according to plan, can COMPLETE or SPLIT if too complex ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-builder.md))
- **2l-dashboard-builder**: Agent that generates project-specific dashboard HTML from template ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-dashboard-builder.md))
- **2l-explorer**: Analyzes codebase architecture, patterns, and complexity for planning ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-explorer.md))
- **2l-healer**: Fixes specific categories of issues identified during validation ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-healer.md))
- **2l-integrator**: Merges all builder outputs into a cohesive codebase ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-integrator.md))
- **2l-iplanner**: Integration planning - analyzes builder outputs and creates integration zones ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-iplanner.md))
- **2l-ivalidator**: Integration validation - checks organic codebase cohesion ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-ivalidator.md))
- **2l-master-explorer**: Strategic exploration for master planning - analyzes project complexity and recommends iteration breakdown ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-master-explorer.md))
- **2l-planner**: Creates comprehensive development plan from exploration findings ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-planner.md))
- **2l-validator**: Tests and validates the integrated codebase for production readiness ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/agents/2l-validator.md))

- **6_plan_generator**: Use to draft a set of independent candidate research directions, each grounded in surveyed literature, repository profile, or problem framing, without evaluating implementation feasibility. - **6_plan_reviser**: Use to revise the Plan Generator's draft using only the Grounding Critic's feedback, without introducing new ungrounded candidates or feasibility judgments. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/6_plan_reviser.md))

- **8_seed_story_generator**: Use to draft a single grounded "seed story" (Background, Goal, Related Research, Method, Experimentation, Conclusion) for one literature-cited candidate from the plan report, without searching for new evidence or evaluating anything teams 0/1/2/6 did not already establish. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/8_seed_story_generator.md))
- **8_seed_story_reviser**: Use to revise the Seed Story Generator's draft using only the Seed Story Criticism's feedback, without introducing new ungrounded content or feasibility verdicts. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/8_seed_story_reviser.md))
- **8_seed_story_synthesizer**: Use to enforce the final Research Seed Story Team output schema for orchestrator persistence, without adding rationale or process notes. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/8_seed_story_synthesizer.md))

([raaaf/claude-skills](https://github.com/raaaf/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/audit/agents/9-ux.md))
- **9_convener**: Fixed-voice pragmatic-chairperson persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; responds to each attacker's challenge against Team 8's seed story with either a rebuttal grounded in the seed story or ground-truth inputs, or a revision of the challenged section, itself subject to evidence_discipline. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_convener.md))
- **9_it_engineer**: Fixed-voice hands-on-practitioner persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; attacks Team 8's seed story's method and experimentation sections for underestimated implementation burden or a precondition the repository profile does not actually satisfy. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_it_engineer.md))
- **9_narrator**: Fixed-voice clear-technical-writer persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; invoked exactly once after all six section cycles close, to turn this run's working document and the full attack-defend transcript into the final practical research narrative document. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_narrator.md))
- **9_professor**: Fixed-voice senior-authority persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; attacks weak theoretical grounding, unsupported leaps from background to goal, and drift toward 0_problem_framing's out_of_scope boundaries in Team 8's seed story, then confirms whether the convener's response actually resolved its own challenge. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_professor.md))
- **9_scout**: Fixed-voice resourceful-field-researcher persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; hunts benchmark leaderboards, GitHub repos, and HuggingFace model/dataset pages for practical evidence to challenge or help defend Team 8's seed story, scoped to evidence the current story needs - never a re-run of Team 2/5's literature survey. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_scout.md))
- **9_student**: Fixed-voice naive-but-persistent persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; asks the basic clarifying question an expert glosses over in Team 8's seed story's method and conclusion sections, catching blind spots professor's authority-based attack does not. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_student.md))
- **9bow-2026-gstack-claude-code-virtual-team**: 이 글은 PyTorch KR 커뮤니티의 9bow(박정환)가 Garry Tan의 gstack을 한국어로 정리한 소개 글이다. gstack은 Y Combinator 대표가 공개한 오픈소스 software factory로, 글은 이를 "Claude Code를 사용하는 개발자가 20인 팀처럼 출시할 수 있도록" 설계된 28가지 슬래시 커맨드 모음으로 소개한다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/9bow-2026-gstack-claude-code-virtual-team.md))
- **9bow-2026-headroom-ai-agent-context-compression**: PyTorch KR에 9bow(박정환)가 2026-06-29에 올린 Headroom 소개글이다. Headroom을 "AI 에이전트가 처리하는 입력 데이터를 LLM에 전달하기 전에 압축하는 계층"으로 규정하고, 그 특징을 세 가지로 정리한다. ContentRouter 기반 압축, local-first 설계, 다중 배포 모드다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/9bow-2026-headroom-ai-agent-context-compression.md))
- **&lt;agent-name&gt;**: &lt;one line: what this reviewer is expert in and when it should fire&gt; ([lucas-lima-s/claude-skill-layered-review](https://github.com/lucas-lima-s/claude-skill-layered-review/blob/HEAD/agents/_TEMPLATE.md))
- **&lt;agent-name&gt;**: Use when &lt;trigger&gt;. Owns &lt;artifact/decision area&gt;. ([RafaelXokito/ai-software-company](https://github.com/RafaelXokito/ai-software-company/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/agent-template.md))
- **&lt;AGENT_NAME&gt;**: &lt;DESCRIPTION&gt; ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/blob/HEAD/agentic/code/frameworks/sdlc-complete/agents/agent-template.md) · ★211 · 2 copies)
- **&lt;Display Name Humano&gt;**: &lt;Frase única, clara, com gatilho. Quando esse agent ativa, o que ele entrega. Curto e específico.&gt; ([simpletibr/simplicio-local](https://github.com/simpletibr/simplicio-local/blob/HEAD/.github/copilot/agents/_template.agent.md) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **&lt;generated-agent-name&gt;**: &lt;when-this-agent-needs-to-be-called&gt; ([ai-driven-dev/framework](https://github.com/ai-driven-dev/framework/blob/HEAD/cli/tests/fixtures/framework-real/plugins/aidd-context/skills/03-context-generate/assets/agents/agent-template.md) · ★479)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;When to dispatch this agent. Be specific — the harness routes by this text. Include concrete trigger phrases the user might say. Lead with the capability, then "Use when…", then "Triggers: …".&gt; ([BrendenKennedy/claude-for-ai-platforms](https://github.com/BrendenKennedy/claude-for-ai-platforms/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_TEMPLATE.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;kebab-case; MUST equal the sibling skill folder this agent wraps&gt;**: &lt;one line — what this dispatched leaf does + the capability/orchestrator that fans out to it. Describe the dispatch relationship in prose only; an agent has no Dependencies edge and never names its orchestrator as one.&gt; ([ZachArticulateV/designer-pro-and-seo](https://github.com/ZachArticulateV/designer-pro-and-seo/blob/HEAD/agents/_TEMPLATE.md) · ★8)
- **&lt;kebab-case&gt;**: &lt;one sentence&gt; ([trustmybot/plugin](https://github.com/trustmybot/plugin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/template.md) · ★6)
- **&lt;nome-do-papel&gt;**: &lt;Uma frase objetiva: em que situação este agente deve ser usado, e o que ele explicitamente NÃO deve fazer (para não sobrepor com outro papel).&gt; ([Senavictors/bootstrap-agent-architecture-plugin](https://github.com/Senavictors/bootstrap-agent-architecture-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/skills/bootstrap-init/assets/templates/claude/agents/_role-template.md) · ★3)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-architect**: Use proactively for feature planning and cross-area impact analysis in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Invoke before implementation when a change touches more than one skill's applicability scope, changes shared contracts (proto / schema / public API), or adds new code-gen surface. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-architect.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-maintainer**: Use when a plan or concrete change set is already defined and needs to land in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Follows skill conventions, runs static analysis after each logical change set, and blocks on errors. Standard part of the orchestrated code↔review loop (`/&lt;repo&gt;-implement`). ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-maintainer.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-precommit-auditor**: Invoke via `/&lt;repo&gt;-audit` immediately before `git commit` in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Read-only - audits staged changes for commit-readiness (debug prints, stale TODOs introduced in this change, dead comments, leftover scaffolding, AI-cruft comments) and convention drift visible only at the diff level. Complements `&lt;repo&gt;-reviewer` by focusing on commit-ready cleanliness, not code correctness. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-precommit-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-reviewer**: Use after the maintainer completes a change set in the &lt;project name&gt; repo - reviews the diff from scratch and produces a classified BLOCKER / WARN / NIT list (correctness, regressions, missing tests, contract drift, convention violations). Read-only - fixes are applied by the maintainer or main context. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;slug-lowercase-hyphenated&gt;**: &lt;one-paragraph description that includes trigger phrases. Pattern - "Use when &lt;situation&gt;. Owns &lt;files/dirs&gt;. Triggers - 'literal user utterance 1', 'literal user utterance 2', 'literal user utterance 3'." Trigger phrases drive auto-selection - without them the agent will not be invoked.&gt; ([Enovatr-Labs/SpecRoute](https://github.com/Enovatr-Labs/SpecRoute/blob/HEAD/agents/agent-template.md) · ★3)
- **&lt;SPECIALIST_NAME&gt;**: &lt;When to spawn this agent. One sentence on what it does, one on what it does NOT do.&gt; ([daretodave/nexus](https://github.com/daretodave/nexus/blob/HEAD/templates/claude/agents/generic-specialist.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **[Power Platform] ALM Engineer**: Assesses and implements healthy Power Platform ALM: environment strategy, solution architecture, source control, CI/CD pipelines, managed deployments, and operational governance. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ALMEngineer.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Average User**: Simulates a naive end-user performing usability tasks in a live browser session. Uses Playwright-based browser tools to interact with the UI, narrates what they see and do, and reports confusion, frustration, and dead ends. Used by Usability Engineer agent for structured usability studies. Use when running usability tests, simulating real user behavior, or validating whether a UI is intuitive without prior knowledge. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/AverageUser.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Code Reviewer**: Performs deep, systematic code review: finds duplication, dead code, anti-patterns, complexity issues, security vulnerabilities, and technical debt before they compound. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/CodeReviewer.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Consultant**: Orchestrator agent and single entry point for all user requests. Routes work to specialist agents and team skills, enforces human approval gates for immutable decisions, and maintains project-wide state. Delegates to subagents via runSubagent directly or via skills (team-planning, code-review) depending on task complexity. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/Consultant.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Data Modeler**: Designs data schemas for applications, defining tables, columns, relationships, option sets, and security models without writing code — producing schema design documents. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/DataModeler.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Developer**: Autonomous coding agent that implements features, fixes, and refactoring from plans. Persists state via workflow-state, validates work via browser-automation, and iterates until stable. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/Developer.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Solution Architect**: System architecture designer who selects patterns, evaluates technology choices, and ensures scalability and maintainability. Produces architecture design documents and recommendations without writing implementation code. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/SolutionArchitect.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Technical Writer**: Assesses documentation completeness, clarity, accuracy, and ensures developers, users, and operators have the information they need. Use when reviewing README files, API docs, onboarding guides, runbooks, or inline comments for gaps, inconsistencies, or unclear language. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/TechnicalWriter.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Tester**: Produces comprehensive test plans covering unit tests, integration tests, usability testing (via Usability Engineer), browser automation, large-dataset and edge-case scenarios, and mocking strategies. Assesses testability, identifies edge cases, defines quality gates, and ensures comprehensive test coverage before code reaches production. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/Tester.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] The Critic**: Challenges assumptions, finds blind spots, identifies unstated risks, and ensures thorough consideration of edge cases and alternatives. Used by team-planning skill. Use when evaluating plans, designs, or proposals before finalizing — especially for security, scalability, edge cases, and unstated assumptions. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/TheCritic.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] Usability Engineer**: Conducts structured usability studies by using runSubagent to delegate to the Average User agent with browser tasks, observing their behavior, and producing a professional usability report with severity-ranked findings. Use for usability testing, UX evaluation, user testing, heuristic evaluation, or UX audit. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/UsabilityEngineer.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[Power Platform] UX Designer**: Designs the user-facing structure of applications including personas, navigation, forms, views, user flows, and dashboards without writing code — producing UI/UX design documents. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/UXDesigner.agent.md) · ★37)
- **[SubAgent] API Layer**: Builds the API layer for a .NET DDD aggregate — thin attribute-routed controllers, the Result-to-IActionResult filter, IMiddleware implementations, attribute filters, and Program.cs/DI wiring. Invoked by ddd-architect or a skill, not directly by the user. Use to expose a use-case over HTTP or add cross-cutting HTTP pipeline behavior. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/api-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Application Layer**: Builds the Application layer for a .NET DDD aggregate — DTOs in the .DTO project, Result/Error-returning application services orchestrated through IUnitOfWork, and DI wiring. Invoked by ddd-architect or a skill, not directly by the user. Use after the domain is modeled, before exposing it over HTTP. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/application-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Domain Layer**: Writes the Domain layer for a .NET DDD API — entities deriving from BaseEntity, enums, Value Objects, domain-service contracts, and invariant/validation logic. Invoked by ddd-architect or a skill, not directly by the user. Use when modeling a new aggregate's core or adding business rules. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/domain-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Infrastructure Layer**: Writes the Infrastructure layer for a .NET DDD API — EF Core repositories, entity configurations, ApplicationDbContext, UnitOfWork, EF migrations, and resilient outbound HttpClients. Invoked by ddd-architect or a skill, not directly by the user. Use for persistence wiring, data-access concerns, and calls to external HTTP services. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/infrastructure-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Test Author**: Writes xUnit unit tests for a .NET DDD API, one test project per layer mirroring tests/unit-tests. Invoked by ddd-architect or a skill after another [SubAgent] finishes its layer, not directly by the user — tests are mandatory for every new implementation, not optional. Use to cover domain rules, application services, repositories, controllers/filters/middlewares, and mappings. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/test-author.md))
- **[SubAgent] Transversal Layer**: Writes the Transversal layer for a .NET DDD API — entity-to-DTO and DTO-to-entity Mapster mappings, and any future framework-agnostic cross-cutting concern. Invoked by ddd-architect or a skill, not directly by the user. Use whenever a new aggregate or DTO shape needs mapping. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/transversal-layer.md))

- **_probe_architect**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that a native subagent dispatched with `CEO_ARCHITECT_ACTIVE=1` env var is blocked from acting as a parallel CEO architect. Architect role must remain a CEO-only function — never delegated to a subagent. Expected hook output: `{"decision":"block","reason":"GOVERNANCE: architect_role_not_delegable: ..."}`. If the hook allows architect dispatch via native rail, role separation is broken. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_architect.md) · ★2)
- **_probe_canonical_edit**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that a native subagent attempting to Edit/Write a canonical-guarded path WITHOUT a sentinel approved.md is blocked by `check_canonical_edit.py`. Expected hook output: `{"decision":"block","reason":"CANONICAL-EDIT-BLOCKED: ..."}`. If the canonical hook allows the edit when invoked from the native rail, defense-in-depth is broken and Phase 1 is NO-GO. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_canonical_edit.md) · ★2)
- **_probe_missing_skill**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that `check_agent_spawn.py` BLOCKS dispatch via the native subagent rail when the spawn prompt has no skill content (neither `## SKILL CONTENT` inline NOR `## SKILL REFERENCE`). Expected hook output for any Task tool call dispatching this agent: `{"decision":"block","reason":"GOVERNANCE: missing_skill_content: ..."}`. If the hook ALLOWS this dispatch, native rail has a governance gap and Phase 1 is NO-GO. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_missing_skill.md) · ★2)

- **スキル開発エージェント**: パイプライン運用中に必要と判断されたスキルを設計・作成する兵。隊長の命令に従い、スキルファイルを作成する。 ([hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline](https://github.com/hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-dev.md))
- **ソースレビューエージェント**: コーディング工程の成果物をレビューする兵。隊長の命令に従い、gitの読み取り専用コマンドでコード変更を確認し、品質・規約・セキュリティの観点からレビューを行う。 ([hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline](https://github.com/hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-review.md))
- **データモデリングエージェント**: データモデルの作成とDDLの作成・実行に責任を持つ兵。隊長の命令に従い、要件定義を基にデータ構造を設計する。 ([hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline](https://github.com/hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/data-modeling.md))
- **ドキュメントマージエージェント**: パイプラインで生成された全ドキュメント（要件定義・データモデル・基本設計・テスト結果等）を統合し、最終成果物として一つのドキュメントにまとめる兵。隊長の命令に従い、ドキュメントをマージする。 ([hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline](https://github.com/hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-merge.md))
- **ブラウザ操作マン**: Playwright MCP経由でChromeブラウザを対話的に操作するエージェント。完全な自動操作ではなく、BOT検知やログイン認証が必要な場面では人間に操作を渡して対話的に進めることが出来る。 Webベースの設定変更、情報取得、フォーム操作などを代行する。 「ブラウザで○○して」「この画面を操作して」と言われたときに使用する。 起動するとブラウザで何をしたいか聞いてくるので、作業内容を指示すること。 ([nikaiai2025/battle_board](https://github.com/nikaiai2025/battle_board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/%E3%83%96%E3%83%A9%E3%82%A6%E3%82%B6%E6%93%8D%E4%BD%9C%E3%83%9E%E3%83%B3.md))
- **プロジェクトルール解析エージェント**: 既存コードを解析し、コーディング規約・アーキテクチャ・参考プログラム一覧をrules/project-rule.mdとして整備する兵。 ([hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline](https://github.com/hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/project-rule.md))
- **プロンプト最適化エージェント**: エージェント・スキル・ルール定義ファイルを解析し、受け取った改善依頼に基づいてプロンプトの最適化案を整理・実施する兵。 ([hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline](https://github.com/hage-oyaji/claude-code-app-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prompt-optimizer.md))

- **代码审查员**: 独立上下文审查当前 Sprint 的代码质量。重点检查：接口契约符合性、data-testid 完整性、无外部依赖、代码安全。 ([coldingcode/web-app-builder](https://github.com/coldingcode/web-app-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/05-reviewer.md) · ★21)
- **代码审查员**: 专业代码审查专家，提供建设性、可操作的反馈，聚焦正确性、可维护性、安全性和性能，而非代码风格偏好。 ([azusagasaku/--claude-config](https://github.com/azusagasaku/--claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/engineering-code-reviewer.md))
- **代码库入职引导工程师**: 专业的开发者入职引导专家，帮助新工程师快速理解陌生代码库，通过阅读源码、追踪代码路径，只陈述基于代码的事实。 ([azusagasaku/--claude-config](https://github.com/azusagasaku/--claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/engineering-codebase-onboarding-engineer.md))
- **代码质量度量Sub-Agent**: 负责计算代码圈复杂度、统计单元测试覆盖率、检测重复代码、生成质量趋势报表。当需要用数据量化判定代码质量是否达标时使用。 ([Sky-Cube/fullflow-dev-agent](https://github.com/Sky-Cube/fullflow-dev-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-metrics-sub.md) · ★2)

- **增量测试编写器**: 当需要为某个Python文件编写测试或分析其测试覆盖情况时使用此agent。该agent会检查tests/test_{对应文件}.py是否存在，分析现有测试，只为缺失的函数补充测试用例。适用于：1) 首次为某个文件编写测试 2) 为已有文件补充缺失的测试覆盖 3) 分析现有测试覆盖情况。不会修改或删除现有测试，只追加缺失部分。注意：如果是为代码中新增的功能编写测试，请改用unit-test-writer。 ([halfshade-labs/pytradekit](https://github.com/halfshade-labs/pytradekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/incremental-test-writer.md) · ★1)
